### Competency 1: Develop data storage strategies using elemental data types in a computer’s volatile memory
:A3.4.2_Competency_1_IS2020 rdf:type owl:NamedIndividual ,
                              :AtomicCompetence ;
                     :hasCompetenceArea :Application_Development_and_Programming ;
                     :hasDescription :LS_IS_A3.4.2_C1_Description ;
                     :hasDisposition :PurposeDriven ,
                                     :Meticulous ,
                                     :Inventive ;
                     :hasIdentifier :LIT_IS_A3.4.2_C1 ;
                     :hasKnowledgeSkill :KS1_IS_A3.4.2_C1 ,
                                        :KS2_IS_A3.4.2_C1 ,
                                        :KS3_IS_A3.4.2_C1 ,
                                        :KS4_IS_A3.4.2_C1 ,
                                        :KS5_IS_A3.4.2_C1 ,
                                        :KS6_IS_A3.4.2_C1 ;
                     :hasTitle :LS_IS_A3.4.2_C1_Title .

### Knowledge Elements
:K1_IS_A3.4.2_C1 rdf:type owl:NamedIndividual ,
                   :ComputingKnowledge ;
          rdfs:label "Select appropriate primitive data types to store and recall numeric, string/character, and Boolean data." .

:K2_IS_A3.4.2_C1 rdf:type owl:NamedIndividual ,
                   :ComputingKnowledge ;
          rdfs:label "Define new derivative data types used to compose complex types from primitive types." .

:K3_IS_A3.4.2_C1 rdf:type owl:NamedIndividual ,
